declare void @willrt_init()
declare void @willrt_shutdown()
define i32 @main() {
entry:
  call void @willrt_init()
  call void @"std/errors.will$init"()
  %t2011 = call ptr @"example.com/concurrency/asyncfile.will$main.task"(i32 -2, i32 0)
  %t2013 = alloca i32
  %t2012 = call i32 @willrt_join(ptr %t2011, ptr %t2013, ptr null)
  %t2014 = icmp ne i32 %t2012, 0
  br i1 %t2014, label %main.async.fail.290, label %main.async.ok.290
main.async.fail.290:
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 1
main.async.ok.290:
  %t2015 = load i32, ptr %t2013
  call void @willrt_shutdown()
  ret i32 %t2015
}
@will.error.data.8 = private constant { ptr, i64, i32 } { ptr @.str.40, i64 8, i32 8 }
define internal { ptr, i64 } @will.error.message.8(ptr %data) {
entry:
  %t2016 = getelementptr inbounds { ptr, i64, i32 }, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 0
  %t2017 = load ptr, ptr %t2016
  %t2018 = getelementptr inbounds { ptr, i64, i32 }, ptr %data, i32 0, i32 1
  %t2019 = load i64, ptr %t2018
  %t2020 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } undef, ptr %t2017, 0
  %t2021 = insertvalue { ptr, i64 } %t2020, i64 %t2019, 1
  ret { ptr, i64 } %t2021
}
define internal i32 @will.error.status.8(ptr %data) {
